Abstract: Firearm violence is a pressing public health issue, yet research into survivors' lived experiences remains underfunded and difficult to scale. Qualitative research, including in-depth interviews, is a valuable tool for understanding the personal and societal consequences of community firearm violence and designing effective interventions. However, manually analyzing these narratives through thematic analysis and inductive coding is time-consuming and labor-intensive. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have opened the door to automating this process, though concerns remain about whether these models can accurately and ethically capture the experiences of vulnerable populations. In this study, we assess the use of open-source LLMs to inductively code interviews with 21 Black men who have survived community firearm violence. Our results demonstrate that while some configurations of LLMs can identify important codes, overall relevance remains low and is highly sensitive to data processing. Furthermore, LLM guardrails lead to substantial narrative erasure. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of LLM-assisted qualitative coding and underscore the ethical challenges of applying AI in research involving marginalized communities.

</details>


### [40] [Sentiment Analysis of German Sign Language Fairy Tales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16138)
*Fabrizio Nunnari,Siddhant Jain,Patrick Gebhard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个用于德国手语童话情感分析的数据集和模型，通过文本情感标注和视频特征提取，训练可解释模型预测手语视频的情感极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是开发一个能够分析德国手语(DGS)童话情感的系统，探索手语中面部和身体动作在情感表达中的相对重要性，填补手语情感分析领域的空白。

Method: 方法包括三个步骤：1) 使用四个大型语言模型对德语童话文本片段进行情感标注（负面、中性、正面），通过多数投票达成标注一致性；2) 使用MediaPipe从对应的DGS视频片段中提取面部和身体动作特征；3) 基于XGBoost训练可解释模型，从视频特征预测情感极性。

Result: 文本标注的标注者间一致性达到Krippendorff's alpha 0.781；视频情感预测模型的平均平衡准确率为0.631；特征重要性分析显示，除了眉毛和嘴巴动作外，臀部、肘部和肩部的动作也对情感区分有显著贡献，表明面部和身体在手语情感传达中同等重要。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了德国手语童话情感分析的数据集和模型，证实了面部和身体动作在手语情感表达中的同等重要性，为手语情感识别提供了可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a dataset and a model for sentiment analysis of German sign language (DGS) fairy tales. First, we perform sentiment analysis for three levels of valence (negative, neutral, positive) on German fairy tales text segments using four large language models (LLMs) and majority voting, reaching an inter-annotator agreement of 0.781 Krippendorff's alpha. Second, we extract face and body motion features from each corresponding DGS video segment using MediaPipe. Finally, we train an explainable model (based on XGBoost) to predict negative, neutral or positive sentiment from video features. Results show an average balanced accuracy of 0.631. A thorough analysis of the most important features reveal that, in addition to eyebrows and mouth motion on the face, also the motion of hips, elbows, and shoulders considerably contribute in the discrimination of the conveyed sentiment, indicating an equal importance of face and body for sentiment communication in sign language.

</details>


### [41] [On the Rejection Criterion for Proxy-based Test-time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16146)
*Ayoub Hammal,Pierre Zweigenbaum,Caio Corro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于保守置信度赌注的新拒绝准则，改进测试时对齐方法，在多个数据集上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时对齐方法存在局限性：隐式奖励方法会扭曲大模型分布，而推动方法基于置信度准则在语言学上不合理（如模糊表达）。需要更合理的拒绝准则来改进对齐效果。

Method: 1. 将现有方法统一为从相似图模型采样，区别仅在于拒绝准则定义；2. 分析置信度准则的局限性；3. 提出基于保守置信度赌注的新拒绝准则

Result: 新方法在多个数据集上实验表现优于先前工作，验证了所提拒绝准则的有效性

Conclusion: 通过统一现有方法框架并引入基于保守置信度赌注的新拒绝准则，有效改进了测试时对齐方法，解决了置信度准则在语言学上的不合理性问题

Abstract: Recent works proposed test-time alignment methods that rely on a small aligned model as a proxy that guides the generation of a larger base (unaligned) model. The implicit reward approach skews the large model distribution, whereas the nudging approach defers the generation of the next token to the small aligned model when the large base one is unconfident about its outcome. In this work, we first show that both approaches can be reduced to sampling from similar graphical models, where they differ only in the definition of a rejection criterion (or distribution). Moreover, we argue that the confidence criterion is ill-motivated due to linguistic phenomena like ambiguous phrasing. We propose a novel rejection criterion based on a conservative confidence bet. Experimentally, our novel approach outperforms previous work on several datasets.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Surface Statistics: Robust Conformal Prediction for LLMs via Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16217)
*Yanli Wang,Peng Kuang,Xiaoyu Han,Kaidi Xu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于内部表征的LLM问答一致性预测框架，使用层间信息分数作为非一致性分数，在分布偏移下比基于输出的方法有更好的有效性-效率权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在可靠性要求高的场景中部署时，基于输出的不确定性信号（如token概率、熵、自一致性）在校准-部署不匹配时变得脆弱。一致性预测在可交换性下提供有限样本有效性，但其实际效用取决于非一致性分数的质量。

Method: 提出LLM问答的一致性预测框架，使用内部表征而非输出统计：引入层间信息（LI）分数，衡量输入条件如何重塑模型深度上的预测熵，并将其用作标准分割一致性管道中的非一致性分数。

Result: 在封闭式和开放域QA基准测试中，特别是在跨域偏移下，该方法比强大的文本级基线实现了更好的有效性-效率权衡，同时在相同名义风险水平下保持竞争力的域内可靠性。

Conclusion: 当表面级不确定性在分布偏移下不稳定时，内部表征可以提供更具信息量的一致性分数，表明内部表征在构建鲁棒不确定性量化方法中的价值。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where reliability matters, yet output-level uncertainty signals such as token probabilities, entropy, and self-consistency can become brittle under calibration--deployment mismatch. Conformal prediction provides finite-sample validity under exchangeability, but its practical usefulness depends on the quality of the nonconformity score. We propose a conformal framework for LLM question answering that uses internal representations rather than output-facing statistics: specifically, we introduce Layer-Wise Information (LI) scores, which measure how conditioning on the input reshapes predictive entropy across model depth, and use them as nonconformity scores within a standard split conformal pipeline. Across closed-ended and open-domain QA benchmarks, with the clearest gains under cross-domain shift, our method achieves a better validity--efficiency trade-off than strong text-level baselines while maintaining competitive in-domain reliability at the same nominal risk level. These results suggest that internal representations can provide more informative conformal scores when surface-level uncertainty is unstable under distribution shift.

</details>


### [43] [Optimizing Korean-Centric LLMs via Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16235)
*Hoyeol Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了通过令牌剪枝（token pruning）压缩的多语言大语言模型在韩语中心NLP任务上的性能，发现剪枝能显著提升生成稳定性并减少词汇量，是内存受限场景的有效优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是评估令牌剪枝这一压缩技术在多语言大语言模型上的效果，特别是在韩语中心自然语言处理任务中。通过消除与目标应用无关语言的令牌和嵌入参数，探索在保持性能的同时减少模型内存占用的可行性。

Method: 方法包括：1）选择Qwen3、Gemma-3、Llama-3和Aya等先进多语言LLM；2）采用三种词汇配置：原始词汇、英语-韩语（EnKo）和英语-韩语-中文（EnKoZh）；3）在韩语中心NLP任务上评估，包括通用能力、文化素养、指令跟随和机器翻译；4）使用标准基准进行系统性能评估。

Result: 结果显示：1）令牌剪枝显著提高了生成稳定性，消除了语言混淆；2）在机器翻译任务中，经常能提升韩语特定任务的性能；3）指令跟随能力显示出与潜在跨语言表示相关的架构依赖性差异；4）词汇量显著减少，验证了令牌剪枝作为内存受限、领域特定部署的有效优化策略，尽管推理延迟的改善有限。

Conclusion: 结论是令牌剪枝是一种高度有效的优化策略，特别适用于内存受限的领域特定部署场景。虽然推理延迟改善有限，但通过消除语言混淆显著提升了生成稳定性，并在韩语特定任务中经常能改善机器翻译性能，同时大幅减少词汇量。

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic benchmark of state-of-the-art multilingual large language models (LLMs) adapted via token pruning - a compression technique that eliminates tokens and embedding parameters corresponding to languages irrelevant to the target application. Focusing on Korean-centric natural language processing (NLP) tasks, we evaluate architectures including Qwen3, Gemma-3, Llama-3, and Aya across three vocabulary configurations: Original, English-Korean (EnKo), and English-Korean-Chinese (EnKoZh). Performance is assessed using established benchmarks for general aptitude, cultural literacy, instruction following, and machine translation. Our findings indicate that token pruning significantly improves generation stability by eliminating language confusion, and in the case of machine translation, frequently enhances performance on Korean-specific tasks. While instruction-following capabilities display architecture-dependent variance linked to latent cross-lingual representations, the significant reduction in vocabulary size validates token pruning as a highly effective optimization strategy for memory-constrained, domain-specific deployments, despite modest gains in inference latency.

</details>


### [44] [BAGEL: Benchmarking Animal Knowledge Expertise in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16241)
*Jiacheng Shen,Masato Hagiwara,Milad Alizadeh,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Marius Miron,David Robinson,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen,Gagan Narula,Mathieu Laurière,Matthieu Geist,Olivier Pietquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BAGEL是一个评估语言模型动物知识专业性的基准测试，通过闭卷评估方式衡量模型在动物相关领域的专业知识，无需外部检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在广泛领域知识和推理基准上表现良好，但尚不清楚它们在统一的闭卷评估协议下处理专业动物知识的能力如何。需要专门基准来评估语言模型在动物相关领域的专业知识。

Method: 从多样化的科学和参考来源（包括bioRxiv、Global Biotic Interactions、Xeno-canto和Wikipedia）构建基准，结合精选示例和自动生成的闭卷问答对。涵盖动物知识的多个方面：分类学、形态学、栖息地、行为、发声、地理分布和物种相互作用。

Result: BAGEL基准支持跨来源领域、分类群组和知识类别的细粒度分析，能够更精确地描述模型优势和系统性失败模式。通过闭卷评估测量模型在推理时无需外部检索的动物相关知识。

Conclusion: BAGEL为研究语言模型在领域特定知识泛化提供了新的测试平台，并有助于提高它们在生物多样性相关应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong performance on broad-domain knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, but it remains unclear how well language models handle specialized animal-related knowledge under a unified closed-book evaluation protocol. We introduce BAGEL, a benchmark for evaluating animal knowledge expertise in language models. BAGEL is constructed from diverse scientific and reference sources, including bioRxiv, Global Biotic Interactions, Xeno-canto, and Wikipedia, using a combination of curated examples and automatically generated closed-book question-answer pairs. The benchmark covers multiple aspects of animal knowledge, including taxonomy, morphology, habitat, behavior, vocalization, geographic distribution, and species interactions. By focusing on closed-book evaluation, BAGEL measures animal-related knowledge of models without external retrieval at inference time. BAGEL further supports fine-grained analysis across source domains, taxonomic groups, and knowledge categories, enabling a more precise characterization of model strengths and systematic failure modes. Our benchmark provides a new testbed for studying domain-specific knowledge generalization in language models and for improving their reliability in biodiversity-related applications.

</details>


### [45] [SwanNLP at SemEval-2026 Task 5: An LLM-based Framework for Plausibility Scoring in Narrative Word Sense Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16262)
*Deshan Sumanathilaka,Nicholas Micallef,Julian Hough,Saman Jayasinghe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的框架，用于评估叙事文本中同形异义词义项的合理性，结合结构化推理机制，通过微调小参数模型和动态少样本提示大参数模型来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在自然语言理解方面取得进展，但在真实叙事语境中评估词义合理性的实际应用仍待探索。SemEval-2026 Task 5旨在填补这一空白，研究LLM在短篇故事中评估人类感知的词义合理性能力。

Method: 提出基于LLM的框架，采用结构化推理机制评估同形异义词义项的合理性。方法包括：1）微调低参数LLM并探索不同推理策略；2）对大参数模型使用动态少样本提示；3）模型集成技术提升性能。

Result: 商业大参数LLM结合动态少样本提示能够接近人类对词义合理性的判断。模型集成略微提升性能，相比单一模型预测，能更好地模拟五位人类标注者的一致性模式。

Conclusion: LLM结合结构化推理机制和动态少样本提示在叙事文本的词义合理性评估任务中表现良好，模型集成能更好地模拟人类标注一致性，为LLM在真实叙事语境中的应用提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in language models have substantially improved Natural Language Understanding (NLU). Although widely used benchmarks suggest that Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively disambiguate, their practical applicability in real-world narrative contexts remains underexplored. SemEval-2026 Task 5 addresses this gap by introducing a task that predicts the human-perceived plausibility of a word sense within a short story. In this work, we propose an LLM-based framework for plausibility scoring of homonymous word senses in narrative texts using a structured reasoning mechanism. We examine the impact of fine-tuning low-parameter LLMs with diverse reasoning strategies, alongside dynamic few-shot prompting for large-parameter models, on accurate sense identification and plausibility estimation. Our results show that commercial large-parameter LLMs with dynamic few-shot prompting closely replicate human-like plausibility judgments. Furthermore, model ensembling slightly improves performance, better simulating the agreement patterns of five human annotators compared to single-model predictions

</details>


### [46] [From Benchmarking to Reasoning: A Dual-Aspect, Large-Scale Evaluation of LLMs on Vietnamese Legal Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16270)
*Van-Truong Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个双方面评估框架，用于评估大语言模型在越南法律文本简化任务中的表现，包括性能基准测试和大规模错误分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南法律文本的复杂性阻碍了公众获取司法公正，大语言模型为法律文本简化提供了有前景的解决方案，但需要超越表面指标的多方面评估方法来真正评估其能力。

Method: 1) 建立性能基准：评估四个最先进的大语言模型（GPT-4o、Claude 3 Opus、Gemini 1.5 Pro和Grok-1）在准确性、可读性和一致性三个关键维度的表现；2) 大规模错误分析：在60个复杂的越南法律条款数据集上，使用专家验证的错误分类法进行深入分析。

Result: 发现关键权衡：Grok-1在可读性和一致性方面表现出色，但在细粒度法律准确性上有所妥协；Claude 3 Opus虽然获得高准确性分数，但掩盖了大量细微但关键的推理错误。错误分析指出"错误示例"和"误解"是最普遍的失败类型，表明当前LLMs的主要挑战不是摘要能力，而是受控、准确的法律推理。

Conclusion: 通过整合定量基准和定性深度分析，该研究为大语言模型在法律应用中的评估提供了全面且可操作的评估框架，揭示了模型在法律文本简化任务中的真实能力和局限性。

Abstract: The complexity of Vietnam's legal texts presents a significant barrier to public access to justice. While Large Language Models offer a promising solution for legal text simplification, evaluating their true capabilities requires a multifaceted approach that goes beyond surface-level metrics. This paper introduces a comprehensive dual-aspect evaluation framework to address this need. First, we establish a performance benchmark for four state-of-the-art large language models (GPT-4o, Claude 3 Opus, Gemini 1.5 Pro, and Grok-1) across three key dimensions: Accuracy, Readability, and Consistency. Second, to understand the "why" behind these performance scores, we conduct a large-scale error analysis on a curated dataset of 60 complex Vietnamese legal articles, using a novel, expert-validated error typology. Our results reveal a crucial trade-off: models like Grok-1 excel in Readability and Consistency but compromise on fine-grained legal Accuracy, while models like Claude 3 Opus achieve high Accuracy scores that mask a significant number of subtle but critical reasoning errors. The error analysis pinpoints \textit{Incorrect Example} and \textit{Misinterpretation} as the most prevalent failures, confirming that the primary challenge for current LLMs is not summarization but controlled, accurate legal reasoning. By integrating a quantitative benchmark with a qualitative deep dive, our work provides a holistic and actionable assessment of LLMs for legal applications.

</details>


### [47] [No Universal Courtesy: A Cross-Linguistic, Multi-Model Study of Politeness Effects on LLMs Using the PLUM Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16275)
*Hitesh Mehta,Arjit Saxena,Garima Chhikara,Rohit Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs对礼貌/不礼貌提示的反应研究：基于礼貌理论，在3种语言、5个模型上测试，发现礼貌提示可提升响应质量约11%，但效果因语言和模型而异，礼貌是可量化的计算变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型对不同礼貌程度用户提示的响应，探索礼貌作为计算变量如何影响LLM行为，以及这种影响是否具有语言和模型依赖性。

Method: 基于Brown和Levinson的礼貌理论及Culpeper的不礼貌框架，在英语、印地语、西班牙语三种语言中，对Gemini-Pro、GPT-4o Mini、Claude 3.7 Sonnet、DeepSeek-Chat和Llama 3五个模型进行实验。使用三种交互历史（原始、礼貌、不礼貌），收集22,500对提示-响应样本，通过包含连贯性、清晰度、深度、响应性、上下文保留、毒性、简洁性和可读性八个因素的评估框架，按五个礼貌级别进行评估。

Result: 模型性能受语气、对话历史和语言显著影响：礼貌提示平均提升响应质量约11%，不礼貌语气则降低质量，但效果不一致且非普遍。英语适合礼貌或直接语气，印地语适合恭敬间接语气，西班牙语适合自信语气。Llama对语气最敏感（11.5%变化范围），GPT对对抗性语气更稳健。礼貌是可量化的计算变量，但其影响具有语言和模型依赖性。

Conclusion: 礼貌显著影响LLM行为，但效果因语言和模型而异，非普遍现象。研究创建了PLUM语料库（1,500个人工验证的多语言礼貌提示），并基于礼貌理论对六个可证伪假设进行了实证分析，为未来研究提供基础。

Abstract: This paper explores the response of Large Language Models (LLMs) to user prompts with different degrees of politeness and impoliteness. The Politeness Theory by Brown and Levinson and the Impoliteness Framework by Culpeper form the basis of experiments conducted across three languages (English, Hindi, Spanish), five models (Gemini-Pro, GPT-4o Mini, Claude 3.7 Sonnet, DeepSeek-Chat, and Llama 3), and three interaction histories between users (raw, polite, and impolite). Our sample consists of 22,500 pairs of prompts and responses of various types, evaluated across five levels of politeness using an eight-factor assessment framework: coherence, clarity, depth, responsiveness, context retention, toxicity, conciseness, and readability. The findings show that model performance is highly influenced by tone, dialogue history, and language. While polite prompts enhance the average response quality by up to ~11% and impolite tones worsen it, these effects are neither consistent nor universal across languages and models. English is best served by courteous or direct tones, Hindi by deferential and indirect tones, and Spanish by assertive tones. Among the models, Llama is the most tone-sensitive (11.5% range), whereas GPT is more robust to adversarial tone. These results indicate that politeness is a quantifiable computational variable that affects LLM behaviour, though its impact is language- and model-dependent rather than universal. To support reproducibility and future work, we additionally release PLUM (Politeness Levels in Utterances, Multilingual), a publicly available corpus of 1,500 human-validated prompts across three languages and five politeness categories, and provide a formal supplementary analysis of six falsifiable hypotheses derived from politeness theory, empirically assessed against the dataset.

</details>

### [48] [Adaptive Regularization within Trust Region Methods for Stochastic Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15457)
*Yunsoo Ha,Sara Shashaani,Quoc Tran-dinh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Reg-ASTRO算法，在目标函数光滑且梯度存在噪声的情况下，实现几乎确定的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$迭代复杂度，样本复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4.5})$，在更强条件下可提升至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3.5})$


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有决策依赖噪声、无界支撑且服从次指数尾分布的非凸随机优化问题，现有方法在迭代复杂度和样本复杂度方面存在不足，需要开发更高效的算法

Method: 提出Reg-ASTRO算法，结合自适应采样信任域优化和自适应正则化局部模型。自适应采样确保估计精度与平稳性度量对齐，接近平稳点的迭代触发更高精度要求。算法处理信任域半径和正则化在梯度估计前未确定的耦合问题

Result: 实现几乎确定的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$迭代复杂度，$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4.5})$样本复杂度，在更强正则性条件和公共随机数使用下提升至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3.5})$，理论和数值实验均优于一阶方法

Conclusion: Reg-ASTRO算法在处理具有决策依赖噪声的非凸随机优化问题上表现出色，显著改进了迭代和样本复杂度，为这类问题提供了高效的解决方案

Abstract: We propose a stochastic nonconvex optimization algorithm that achieves almost sure $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$ iteration complexity for problems with smooth objective functions and gradients only observable with noise. The mean-zero stochastic noise is decision-dependent and has unbounded support with subexponential tail, allowing our framework to cover a broad class of problems. The improved almost sure iteration complexity is achieved with a new variant of the adaptive sampling trust-region optimization (ASTRO) augmented with an adaptively regularized local model, which we term Reg-ASTRO. Adaptive sampling ensures that the estimation precision is aligned with a measure of stationarity, so that iterates closer to stationarity trigger higher accuracy requirement for sampling. A key analytical challenge arises because the trust-region radius and regularization are coupled and not determined prior to gradient estimation at each iteration. We further establish an almost sure $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4.5})$ sample complexity for Reg-ASTRO, which improves to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3.5})$ under stronger regularity conditions and use of common random numbers, substantially outperforming first-order methods in theory and numerical experiments.

</details>


### [49] [Enhancing Model Based Derivative Free Optimization using Direct Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15492)
*Zijun Li,Aswin Kannan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个切换框架，将直接搜索方法与基于模型的优化器结合，提升仿真优化性能，并在单目标优化中证明收敛性，在机器学习多目标问题中应用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仿真优化中，基于模型的方法（如信任域法、贝叶斯优化）和搜索方法（如遗传算法、直接搜索）通常孤立使用。本文旨在结合两者的优势，通过切换框架提升优化性能，特别是在机器学习多目标优化场景中。

Method: 提出一个切换框架，利用直接搜索方法来增强任何基于模型的优化器。在单目标设置中分析并证明了切换方法的渐近收敛性。针对机器学习应用，引入预热启动机制，利用先前超参数或架构配置的权重来加速训练。方法在标准CUTEr测试问题和机器学习任务上进行评估。

Result: 方法在标准CUTEr测试问题上与经典贝叶斯和信任域求解器相比表现出持续强大的数值性能。在机器学习任务中，该方法能够有效处理分类和回归问题中的多目标优化，包括准确性、计算时间、算法偏差和稀疏性等目标。

Conclusion: 提出的切换框架成功结合了直接搜索和基于模型优化的优势，在单目标优化中具有理论收敛保证，在多目标机器学习应用中表现出色，为仿真优化提供了有前景的新方法。

Abstract: We consider single and multiobjective simulation-based optimization problems. Simulation-based optimization has traditionally used both model-based and search-based methods, often in isolation. Model-based methods include trust region approaches and Bayesian optimization, while search methods include genetic algorithms and Direct Search-type techniques. In this work, we propose a switching framework that leverages Direct Search methods to enhance the performance of any model-based optimizer. Our contributions are twofold. First, in the single-objective setting, we analyze and prove the asymptotic convergence of the proposed switching approach. Second, motivated by applications in machine learning, we consider both classification and regression problems, where the objectives span accuracy, computational time, algorithmic bias, and sparsity. The models range from complex neural networks and decision trees to simpler KNN-type baselines. For machine learning in particular, we also introduce a warm-starting mechanism using weights from previous hyperparameter or architectural configurations to exploit problem structure and accelerate training. Finally, beyond ML tasks, we evaluate the method on the standard CUTEr test problems and compare its performance against classical Bayesian and trust region solvers. We observe consistently strong numerical performance, suggesting promise for the proposed switching-based approach.

</details>


### [50] [Complexity Guarantees for Zeroth-order Methods via Exponentially-shifted Gaussian Smoothing: Mitigating Dimension-dependence and Incorporating Decision-dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15525)
*Mingrui Wang,Prakash Chakraborty,Uday V. Shanbhag*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出新的指数偏移高斯平滑(esGs)估计器，解决随机零阶优化中的维度依赖问题，实现线性维度依赖而非二次依赖，并扩展到决策依赖场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非光滑随机优化中的两个关键挑战：1) 高斯平滑零阶方法对维度的强烈依赖阻碍大规模应用；2) 缺乏统一的平滑随机零阶方法分析框架，无法同时处理标准和决策依赖的随机优化问题

Method: 引入指数偏移高斯平滑(esGs)估计器，其矩界具有线性维度依赖；将该估计器扩展到决策依赖场景（密度函数已知或未知）；开发esGs启发的随机零阶方法，适用于非光滑强凸、凸和非凸优化

Result: 迭代复杂度维度依赖改善n倍（线性vs二次），保持相似oracle复杂度；凸设置下提供新高概率保证和几乎确定次线性收敛率；非凸设置下提供新的子序列几乎确定收敛保证；初步数值实验显示计算时间改善和精度提升

Conclusion: esGs估计器显著改善了随机零阶优化中的维度依赖问题，为大规模非光滑随机优化提供了更高效的解决方案，并在理论和数值上均表现出优越性能

Abstract: In this paper, we consider two distinct challenges in the resolution of nonsmooth stochastic optimization. Of these, the first pertains to the pronounced dependence of dimension in Gaussian smoothing-enabled zeroth-order schemes, impeding applications to large-scale settings. Second, no unified analysis {exists} for smoothing-enabled stochastic zeroth-order methods, allowing for capturing standard and decision-dependent stochastic optimization. To contend with the first challenge, we introduce a new exponentially-shifted Gaussian smoothing {\bf esGs} estimator whose moment bounds enjoy a linear dependence on dimension (rather than a quadratic dependence as in standard Gaussian smoothing estimators). Second, we show that such an estimator can be extended in two distinct ways to address decision-dependent regimes where the underlying densities are either available in closed form or not. Notably, the resulting gradient estimators continue to display a linear dependence in dimension. We then develop an {\bf esGs}-enabled stochastic zeroth-order method applicable to nonsmooth strongly convex, convex, and {non-}convex regimes. Importantly, our guarantees provide improved dimension-dependence in iteration complexities (by a factor of problem dimension $n$) while maintaing similar oracle complexities. In addition, we also provide novel high probability guarantees and almost sure sublinear rate guarantees in convex settings, while a new subsequential almost sure convergence guarantee is provided in nonconvex regimes. Preliminary numerics support our theoretical findings show that the proposed schemes display improved computational times and more refined empirical accuracies.

</details>


### [51] [Robust Accelerated Adaptive Search: High-Probability Complexity Bounds under Bounded-Moment Stochastic Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15526)
*Shunzhi Zhang,Shichen Liao,Congying Han,Tiande Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出RAAS方法，在具有持续偏差和重尾噪声的随机一阶/零阶优化中，通过可调动量干预实现鲁棒加速自适应搜索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在仅满足有限矩约束的随机一阶/零阶oracle下的无约束光滑凸优化问题，这种环境天然存在持续偏差和重尾噪声。传统方法中动量会传播oracle误差，破坏局部搜索的稳定效果

Method: 提出RAAS（鲁棒加速自适应搜索）方法，采用可调动量干预机制，开发了自适应搜索方法在随机oracle反馈下的高概率分析框架

Result: 建立了强凸和一般凸情况下的高概率停止时间复杂度界，阐明了算法参数如何在早期加速和后期稳定性之间权衡，并提出了有效的切换启发式策略

Conclusion: RAAS方法在敌对优化环境中成功解决了动量传播误差的结构性挑战，实现了加速与稳定的平衡，为随机优化提供了鲁棒的理论保证

Abstract: We study unconstrained smooth convex optimization under stochastic first- and zeroth-order oracles subject only to finite-moment bounds, naturally admitting persistent bias and heavy-tailed noise. In this hostile environment, integrating momentum into \emph{adaptive step search} to secure acceleration poses an inherent structural challenge, because momentum propagates oracle errors across iterations, inevitably undermining the stabilizing effect of local search.
  To address this difficulty, we propose \texttt{RAAS}, a robust accelerated adaptive search method with tunable momentum intervention. Theoretically, we develop a general high-probability framework for adaptive search methods under stochastic oracle feedback, and instantiate it through the strongly convex and general convex analyses of \texttt{RAAS}. This yields high-probability stopping-time complexity bounds for reaching the attainable precision neighborhood. The resulting guarantees also clarify how the algorithmic parameters trade off early-stage acceleration against late-stage stability, and motivate a simple switching heuristic that performs well empirically.

</details>


### [52] [A bi-level priority sorting framework for flexible AGV service scheduling in smart warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15572)
*Xiaozhu Sun,Bilal Farooq*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出双层优化框架协调智能独立仓库中AGV柔性作业，平衡高吞吐量订单履行与严格成本控制，通过动态调度参数调整和实时路径优化，结合启发式规则和强化学习，显著降低订单延迟和系统成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能独立仓库中AGV柔性作业的关键挑战：在保持高吞吐量订单履行的同时实现严格的成本控制，协调柔性客户服务水平、系统成本和运营效率的优化。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：第一层基于预定义客户优先级类别动态调整实时调度参数（订单承诺时间、延迟容忍度）；第二层为每个AGV执行实时路径优化，寻找最短可行路径并避免冲突。针对复杂多容量包裹拣选任务，应用PDSP和DCSP两种启发式规则，并使用A*引导深度Q学习（AGDQN）进行训练。

Result: 综合仿真实验表明，在高峰需求期间，该框架结合两种启发式规则可将平均订单延迟和总系统成本降低50%以上，同时保持90%以上的服务水平并最大化AGV利用率。在正常和波动需求场景下均表现出优越的灵活性和持续效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的双层优化框架有效协调了AGV柔性作业，在平衡订单履行吞吐量和成本控制方面取得显著成效。优先级排序机制在各种强化学习基准测试中展现出稳健的性能优势，为智能仓库运营提供了高效灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a bi-level optimization framework to coordinate Automated Guided Vehicle (AGV) flexible operations in smart independent warehouses, addressing the critical challenge of balancing high-throughput order fulfillment with stringent cost control. The framework is designed to simultaneously optimize flexible customer service level, system cost, and operational efficiency. The first level dynamically adjusts real-time scheduling parameters, such as order commitment times and delay tolerance, based on predefined customer priority categories. The second level performs real-time routing optimization for each AGV by identifying the shortest feasible paths while avoiding conflicts. For complex multi-capacity package picking tasks, two heuristic rules, priority, deadline, with shortest path (PDSP) and delay cost with shortest path (DCSP), are applied to multi-capacity package picking tasks and further training is carried out using the reinforcement learning algorithm of A* guided deep Q-learning (AGDQN). Comprehensive simulation experiments, conducted across diverse warehouse layouts and order demand patterns, demonstrate that the proposed framework equipped with both heuristic rules consistently reduces average order delay and total system costs by over 50% during peak demand periods. This is achieved while maintaining a service level above 90% and maximizing AGV utilization. The method also exhibits superior flexibility and sustained efficiency under normal and fluctuating demand scenarios. Additional ablation studies confirm that the proposed priority sorting mechanism delivers robust performance advantages when tested with various other reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [53] [Housing Decisions under Mobility Risk: A Stochastic Threshold Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15580)
*Hui Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 开发了一个考虑高流动性风险的住房产权选择随机边界模型，推导出价格租金比的最优买卖边界闭式解，解释跨市场异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高流动性风险环境（如军事基地附近）下的住房产权决策问题，解释为何相同的价格租金比在不同地区可能对应不同的最优决策。

Method: 建立随机自由边界模型，假设房价和租金遵循相关扩散过程，家庭面临不确定的搬迁时间。推导出价格租金比的最优买卖边界闭式解。

Result: 流动性风险通过缩短有效持有期和增加不确定性来降低所有权价值，导致相同价格租金比在不同地区对应不同最优决策。数值模拟显示波动率和搬迁强度变化会移动决策阈值。

Conclusion: 该模型为分析不确定性下的住房决策提供了可处理工具，并为高流动性环境中的价格租金比提供了结构性解释，有助于理解跨市场异质性。

Abstract: We develop a stochastic free-boundary model of housing tenure decisions in markets with high mobility risk, such as areas near military installations. Housing prices and rents follow correlated diffusion processes, and households face an uncertain relocation horizon. We derive a closed-form characterization of the optimal buy-versus-rent boundary in terms of the price-to-rent ratio. The model highlights how mobility risk reduces the value of ownership by shortening the effective holding period and increasing uncertainty. As a result, identical price-to-rent ratios can imply different optimal decisions across locations. Numerical illustrations show how variations in volatility and relocation intensity shift the threshold, providing a structural interpretation of observed cross-market heterogeneity. The framework offers a tractable tool for analyzing housing decisions under uncertainty and for interpreting price-to-rent ratios in high-mobility environments.

</details>


### [54] [Scalable Outer Approximation of Minkowski Sums of Matrix Ellipsoids for Data-Driven Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15586)
*Taira Kaminaga,Hampei Sasahara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多矩阵椭球Minkowski和的最优外逼近问题，提出了两种LMI-free的高效求解方法：针对半轴平方和准则的解析解和针对体积准则的MM算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列观测的噪声模型通常表示为多个矩阵椭球的Minkowski和，而现有鲁棒控制方法通常假设单一椭球集，因此需要紧致的外逼近。基于LMI的方法计算成本随数据长度呈二次增长，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 引入参数化的边界矩阵椭球族，针对半轴平方和准则推导精确解析解，针对体积准则开发高效的MM算法。MM算法采用对数行列式函数的一阶近似，提供闭式更新规则，确保单调收敛到驻点集。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法比标准内点求解器具有显著更高的计算效率和可扩展性，能够有效处理大规模数据驱动的控制问题。

Conclusion: 论文提出了两种高效求解多矩阵椭球Minkowski和外逼近问题的方法，解决了现有LMI方法计算复杂度高、可扩展性差的问题，为数据驱动控制中的不确定性建模提供了实用的计算工具。

Abstract: Matrix ellipsoids provide a standard framework for representing bounded uncertainties in data-driven control. Since noise models for sequential observations are naturally represented as the Minkowski sum of multiple matrix ellipsoids, applying existing robust control methods, which typically assume a single ellipsoidal set, requires a tight outer approximation. While techniques based on linear matrix inequalities (LMI) are applicable, their computational cost grows quadratically with the data length, limiting their scalability. This paper investigates the optimal outer approximation problem under two criteria: the sum of squared semi-axes and the volume. We propose an LMI-free approach by introducing a parameterized family of bounding matrix ellipsoids. Specifically, we derive an exact analytical solution for the first criterion and develop an efficient majorization-minimization (MM) algorithm for the second. The proposed MM algorithm employs a first-order approximation of the log-determinant function to provide closed-form update rules, ensuring monotonic convergence to the set of stationary points. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method offers significantly higher computational efficiency and scalability than standard interior-point solvers.

</details>


### [55] [Weakly-Coupled Multi-Action Restless Bandits -- Exponential Convergence in Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15683)
*Jing Fu,Bill Moran,Jose Nino-Mora*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有多组多动作有限状态不安定老虎机过程的有限时间马尔可夫决策过程，证明在系统规模趋于无穷时，一般策略类中的随机过程以指数速率收敛到确定性过程，并提出一种能指数收敛到最优性的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注特定策略/算法，缺乏对一般随机过程行为的分析，特别是缺乏能保证随着问题规模增大收敛到理想轨迹的策略设计理论。需要建立更一般的收敛理论，不依赖非退化假设，并设计能保证指数收敛到最优性的策略。

Method: 研究包含多组多动作有限状态不安定老虎机过程的MDP，各组内老虎机过程相同但组间可不同。考虑状态和动作变量的多重弱耦合约束。证明对于相当一般策略类中的任何策略，当系统规模（老虎机数量）趋于无穷时，随机过程以指数速率依概率收敛到确定性过程。提出一种新策略并证明其在一般情况下能以指数速率依概率收敛到最优性。

Result: 证明了对于一般策略类中的任何策略，随机过程以指数速率依概率收敛到确定性过程，且该指数收敛不依赖任何非退化假设。策略在尺寸维度上渐近接近最优性（次优性指数衰减）当且仅当确定性过程与最优性重合。提出的新策略在一般情况下能以指数速率依概率收敛到最优性。

Conclusion: 建立了不安定老虎机过程在系统规模趋于无穷时的指数收敛理论框架，不依赖非退化假设。证明了策略渐近最优性的充要条件，并提出了一种能保证指数收敛到最优性的策略设计方法，为大规模不安定老虎机问题提供了理论基础和算法保证。

Abstract: We study a finite time horizon Markov decision process (MDP) consisting of several groups of multi-action finite-state restless bandit processes, which are identical within each group. The bandit processes into different groups can be rather different. The bandit processes are subject to multiple weakly coupled constraints on their state and action variables. In contrast to prior studies that considered only a few specific policies/algorithms, here, we study the behaviours of the general stochastic process and, most importantly, the design of policies that guarantee its convergence to an ideal trajectory as the problem size increases. We prove that, for any policy in a rather general class, the resulting stochastic process converges in probability to a deterministic process as the system size (measured by the number of bandits) tends to infinity, at an exponential rate. Unlike the previous proofs, our exponential convergence does not rely on any non-degenerate assumptions. It follows that the chosen policy asymptotically approaches optimality (with exponentially diminishing suboptimality) in the size dimension if and only if the deterministic process coincides with optimality. We further propose a policy and prove that, in general, it converges in probability to optimality exponentially fast in the system size.

</details>


### [56] [Optimization Workshop Notes for Mathematical Programming with Equilibrium Constraints Algorithms: Penalty Interior-Point, Implicit-Programming, and Piecewise SQP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15690)
*Jiguang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研讨会讨论了四种处理带均衡约束的数学规划(MPEC)的算法，重点分析各方法的模型、搜索方向子问题、全局化机制和收敛结果的实际含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPEC是包含下层均衡系统的优化问题，通常通过互补性或变分不等式条件表示。这种结构破坏了标准非线性规划方法所依赖的光滑流形或凸结构，因此需要专门算法来处理这类特殊问题。

Method: 讨论了四种算法视角：1)经典罚内点算法(PIPA)；2)控制互补性衰减的单调线性互补问题(LCP)变体PIPA；3)变分不等式约束MPEC的隐式规划下降法；4)在局部选择的光滑片段上应用SQP的分段SQP(PSQP)方法。

Result: 对每种方法详细解释了其模型、搜索方向子问题、全局化机制和收敛结果的含义，特别强调假设条件的实际作用和算法思想与完整收敛定理之间的区别。

Conclusion: 研讨会系统分析了MPEC问题的多种算法途径，强调理解算法假设的实际意义和区分有吸引力的算法思想与完整收敛定理的重要性，为处理这类特殊优化问题提供了全面的算法视角。

Abstract: In this workshop, we discuss several algorithms for mathematical programs with equilibrium constraints (MPECs). The unifying theme is that MPECs are optimization problems whose feasible set contains a lower-level equilibrium system, often written through complementarity or variational-inequality conditions. This destroys the smooth manifold or convex structure that standard nonlinear programming methods rely on. We focus on four algorithmic viewpoints:
  (i) the classical penalty interior-point algorithm (PIPA); (ii) a monotone-linear complementarity problem (LCP) variant of PIPA that explicitly controls complementarity decay; (iii) an implicit-programming descent method for variational inequality (VI)-constrained MPECs; (iv) piecewise SQP (PSQP), which applies SQP on locally selected smooth pieces.
  For each method we explain the model, the search direction subproblem, the globalization mechanism, and the meaning of the convergence result. Particular emphasis is placed on what the assumptions are really doing and on the distinction between an attractive algorithmic idea and a fully valid convergence theorem.

</details>


### [57] [Finite-Dimensional MOR-Based RHC for Steering 2D Navier-Stokes Equations to Desired Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15846)
*Behzad Azmi,Stefan Frei,Felix Sauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了通过后退时域控制实现二维Navier-Stokes方程到给定参考轨迹的局部指数稳定化，采用有限个执行器的线性组合作为控制，建立了局部指数稳定性和次优性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Navier-Stokes方程的稳定控制问题，特别是通过后退时域控制方法实现对参考轨迹的局部指数稳定化，解决流体控制中的计算复杂性和实时实现难题。

Method: 采用后退时域控制方法，控制由有限个执行器的线性组合实现，执行器由控制子域上的指示函数表示。提出了基于本征正交分解的模型降阶RHC方法以降低计算成本。

Result: 建立了RHC方案的局部指数稳定性和次优性理论结果。数值实验验证了理论发现，并展示了降阶RHC方法在保持良好闭环稳定性能的同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 后退时域控制方法能有效实现二维Navier-Stokes方程的局部指数稳定化，基于本征正交分解的模型降阶方法显著提高了计算效率，为复杂流体控制问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the local exponential stabilization of the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations to a given reference trajectory by means of receding horizon control (RHC). The control is realized as a linear combination of finitely many actuators, represented by indicator functions supported on subsets of a prescribed control subdomain. We establish local exponential stabilizability and suboptimality for the resulting RHC scheme. Numerical experiments for two flow configurations of increasing complexity illustrate the theoretical findings and assess the practical performance of the method. In addition, we propose a model-order-reduced RHC approach based on proper orthogonal decomposition, which significantly reduces the computational cost while maintaining favorable closed-loop stabilization performance in the numerical experiments.

</details>


### [58] [An exact algorithm for vehicle routing problems with temporal dependency constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16064)
*Loek van Montfort,Markus Leitner,Rosario Paradiso*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种处理车辆路径问题中时间依赖关系的精确求解方法，能够处理文献中所有类型的时间依赖关系，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户访问之间的时间依赖关系（如同步约束）是车辆路径问题的基本挑战，这些依赖关系出现在家庭医疗保健路由、飞机调度和技术人员路由等应用中，引入了路径间约束，使问题求解难度显著增加。

Method: 基于片段化表示方法，将路径表示为新型片段的序列，专门设计用于处理时间依赖约束。采用价格-切割-枚举算法，通过交替列生成和行生成计算下界，获取初始上界，并通过片段枚举和分支切割迭代优化上下界，支持多种新型有效不等式。

Result: 计算实验表明，该方法显著优于最先进的基准方法，能够解决先前难以处理的实例，同时覆盖更广泛的时间依赖关系类型。

Conclusion: 提出的精确求解方法为车辆路径问题中的时间依赖关系提供了通用且高效的解决方案，突破了现有方法仅针对特定子类的局限性，在求解能力和适用范围方面均有显著提升。

Abstract: Temporal dependencies between customer visits, such as synchronization constraints, pose a fundamental challenge in vehicle routing. These dependencies, which arise in applications such as home healthcare routing, aircraft scheduling, and technician routing, introduce inter-route constraints that make the resulting problems significantly harder to solve. We present an exact solution method for vehicle routing problems with temporal dependencies capable of handling all types of temporal dependencies studied in the literature, unlike most existing approaches that target specific subclasses. Our approach is based on a fragment-based formulation in which routes are represented as sequences of a new type of fragment, designed to handle temporal dependency constraints. This formulation is solved via a price-cut-and-enumerate algorithm that computes a lower bound using alternating column-and-row generation, obtains an initial upper bound, and iteratively refines both bounds through fragment enumeration and branch-and-cut, supported by several new classes of valid inequalities. Computational experiments show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art benchmark methods and is able to solve previously intractable instances while covering a wider range of temporal dependencies.

</details>


### [59] [Empirical Asymptotic Runtime Analysis of Linear Programming Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16192)
*Edward Rothberg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文实证研究了线性规划问题中三种主要算法（单纯形法、内点法、PDHG）在不同应用领域的渐近运行时增长率，使用大语言模型生成合成但现实的实例进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是了解不同线性规划算法在解决大规模困难LP模型时的渐近运行时表现，为未来大规模LP问题求解选择最有效算法提供依据。

Method: 方法包括：1) 选择三种主流LP算法（单纯形法、内点法、PDHG）；2) 从六个已知产生大规模困难LP模型的优化应用领域中选择实例；3) 使用大语言模型生成合成但现实的实例家族；4) 通过回归模型分析运行时数据。

Result: 结果表明：1) 简单回归模型通常能很好预测同一模型类别内的观察运行时；2) 不同算法间的渐近行为差异显著；3) 这些发现对未来大规模LP模型求解的算法选择有重要影响。

Conclusion: 结论是不同LP算法的渐近运行时增长率存在显著差异，这对未来大规模线性规划问题的算法选择具有重要指导意义，需要根据具体应用领域和模型特性选择最合适的算法。

Abstract: This paper takes an empirical look at asymptotic runtime growth rates for the most widely used algorithms for solving linear programming (LP) problems across a set of six optimization application areas that are known to produce large and difficult LP models. On the algorithm side, we consider the simplex method, interior-point methods, and PDHG. On the model side, we use a large language model (LLM) to create families of instances in different application areas, allowing us to study model types and sizes that are simultaneously synthetic and realistic. The results indicate that simple regression models typically predict observed runtimes quite well within a model class, and that asymptotic behavior can vary significantly between the different algorithms. This may have a significant impact on which algorithms will be most effective for solving large LP models in the future.

</details>

### [60] [The Spectral Geometry of Thought: Phase Transitions, Instruction Reversal, Token-Level Dynamics, and Perfect Correctness Prediction in How Transformers Reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15350)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在推理与事实回忆任务中，其隐藏激活空间表现出谱相变现象，揭示了思维几何的普适方向性、架构特异性动态及结果可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型在推理与事实回忆任务中隐藏激活空间的谱特性差异，建立统一的谱理论来解释模型内部表示的变化规律。

Method: 对11个模型（涵盖5个架构家族）进行系统谱分析，包括Qwen、Pythia、Phi、Llama、DeepSeek-R1，通过计算谱指数α等指标，分析模型在推理与事实回忆任务中的激活空间变化。

Result: 发现七个核心现象：推理谱压缩、指令调优谱反转、架构依赖的生成分类、谱缩放定律、token级谱级联、推理步骤谱标点、谱正确性预测，其中谱指数α在预测正确性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 建立了Transformer的谱推理理论，表明思维几何具有方向普适性、架构特异性动态和结果可预测性，为理解模型内部推理机制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We discover that large language models exhibit \emph{spectral phase transitions} in their hidden activation spaces when engaging in reasoning versus factual recall. Through systematic spectral analysis across \textbf{11 models} spanning \textbf{5 architecture families} (Qwen, Pythia, Phi, Llama, DeepSeek-R1), we identify \textbf{seven} core phenomena: (1)~\textbf{Reasoning Spectral Compression} -- 9/11 models show significantly lower $α$ for reasoning ($p < 0.05$), with larger effects in stronger models; (2)~\textbf{Instruction Tuning Spectral Reversal} -- base models show reasoning $α< $ factual $α$, while instruction-tuned models reverse this relationship; (3)~\textbf{Architecture-Dependent Generation Taxonomy} -- prompt-to-response shifts partition into expansion, compression, and equilibrium regimes; (4)~\textbf{Spectral Scaling Law} -- $α_\text{reasoning} \propto -0.074 \ln N$ across 4 Qwen base models ($R^2 = 0.46$); (5)~\textbf{Token-Level Spectral Cascade} -- per-token alpha tracking reveals local synchronization that decays exponentially with layer distance, and is weaker for reasoning than factual tasks; (6)~\textbf{Reasoning Step Spectral Punctuation} -- phase-transition signatures align with reasoning step boundaries; and (7)~\textbf{Spectral Correctness Prediction} -- spectral $α$ alone achieves AUC $= 1.000$ (Qwen2.5-7B, late layers) and mean AUC $= 0.893$ across 6 models in predicting correctness \emph{before} the final answer is generated. Together, these findings establish a comprehensive \emph{spectral theory of reasoning} in transformers, revealing that the geometry of thought is universal in direction, architecture-specific in dynamics, and predictive of outcome.

</details>


### [61] [Sequential KV Cache Compression via Probabilistic Language Tries: Beyond the Per-Vector Shannon Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15356)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出序列KV压缩方法，利用语言模型的结构特性，通过概率前缀去重和预测差分编码实现比现有向量量化方法高几个数量级的压缩比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化方法（如TurboQuant）已达到每个向量压缩的香农熵极限，但这针对的是较弱的问题。KV缓存中的token来自模型训练的形式语言，模型本身是该语言的近最优预测器，可以利用这一结构特性实现更高效的序列压缩。

Method: 提出两层架构的序列KV压缩：1) 概率前缀去重：使用概率语言trie的度量d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s')识别跨会话的语义等价共享前缀；2) 预测差分编码：仅存储每个新KV向量与模型自身预测之间的残差，实现每个token的熵界H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i})。

Result: 在典型语言模型困惑度（约10-20）下，该方法平均每个token位置仅需3.3-4.3比特，而TurboQuant需要每个向量分量3比特（典型注意力头有64-128个分量）。理论压缩比相对于TurboQuant约为914,000倍。即使在熵底1000倍以上的悲观最坏情况下，压缩比仍保持约914倍，且随着上下文长度增加而改善。

Conclusion: 序列KV压缩通过利用语言模型的结构特性，实现了比现有向量量化方法高几个数量级的压缩效率。该方法的两层架构与现有向量量化方法正交且可组合，为KV缓存压缩提供了新的理论框架和实际改进空间。

Abstract: Recent work on KV cache quantization, culminating in TurboQuant, has approached the Shannon entropy limit for per-vector compression of transformer key-value caches. We observe that this limit applies to a strictly weaker problem than the one that actually matters: compressing the KV cache as a sequence. The tokens stored in a KV cache are not arbitrary floating-point data -- they are samples from the exact formal language the model was trained on, and the model is by construction a near-optimal predictor of that language. We introduce sequential KV compression, a two-layer architecture that exploits this structure. The first layer, probabilistic prefix deduplication, identifies semantically equivalent shared prefixes across sessions using the trie metric d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s') from Probabilistic Language Tries (PLTs). The second layer, predictive delta coding, stores only the residual of each new KV vector from the model's own prediction of it, achieving a per-token entropy bound of H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i}). We prove that at typical language model perplexity -- approximately 10-20 for fluent English text -- this bound is 3.3-4.3 bits on average per token position, compared to TurboQuant's 3 bits per vector component (with typical attention heads having 64-128 components). The theoretical compression ratio over TurboQuant is approximately 914,000x at the Shannon limit. Even at 1000x above the entropy floor -- a deliberately pessimistic worst-case overhead, two orders of magnitude above the 2-5x typical of practical source coders -- the ratio remains approximately 914x over TurboQuant, with compression improving rather than degrading as context length grows. The two layers are orthogonal and compose with existing per-vector quantization methods including TurboQuant.

</details>


### [62] [Mapping High-Performance Regions in Battery Scheduling across Data Uncertainty, Battery Design, and Planning Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15360)
*Jaime de Miguel Rodriguez,Artjom Vargunin,Brigitta Robin Raudne,David Solis Martin,Yaroslava Mykhailenko,Kaarel Oja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过三元分析探索多阶段模型预测控制下储能运行，揭示数据特征、预测不确定性、规划时域和电池倍率之间的相互作用，发现有效时域概念，为工业储能运行提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解储能系统在多阶段模型预测控制下运行时，数据特征、预测不确定性、规划时域和电池倍率等参数之间的复杂相互作用，以优化储能运行策略并降低计算成本。

Method: 采用三元分析方法，通过生成合成数据集系统性地探索数据剖面和不确定性的变化，实现参数化并构建这些特征与最优时域长度之间的映射关系。

Result: 研究发现存在"有效时域"——超过该时域长度后，额外的预测信息对运行效益有限；确定了不同电池类型、不确定性水平和数据剖面组合下的最优时域长度；量化了预测不确定性导致的收益损失，表明即使对快速电池也有影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为工业储能运行提供了实用指导，通过考虑有效时域可以降低计算成本同时保持最优性能；框架为未来机器学习方法奠定了基础，支持工业环境中无需大量计算的连续优化。

Abstract: This study presents a triadic analysis of energy storage operation under multi-stage model predictive control, investigating the interplay between data characteristics, forecast uncertainty, planning horizon, and battery c-rate. Synthetic datasets are generated to systematically explore variations in data profiles and uncertainty, enabling parametrization and the construction of relationships that map these characteristics to optimal horizon length. Results reveal the presence of an effective horizon, defined as the look-ahead length beyond which additional forecast information provides limited operational benefit. Accounting for this horizon can reduce computational costs while maintaining optimal performance. The study provides optimal horizon lengths across a broad range of combinations of battery types, uncertainty levels, and data profiles, offering practical guidance for industrial storage operation. It also quantifies revenue losses due to forecast uncertainty, showing that errors can impact performance even for fast batteries. Finally, the framework lays the groundwork for future machine learning approaches that map dataset parametrization to optimal horizons, supporting continuous optimization in industrial settings without heavy computation.

</details>


### [63] [M3R: Localized Rainfall Nowcasting with Meteorology-Informed MultiModal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15377)
*Sanjeev Panta,Rhett M Morvant,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M3R：一种基于气象信息的多模态注意力架构，通过融合NEXRAD雷达图像和个人气象站测量数据，实现直接降雨预测，在准确性和效率上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的降雨临近预报对灾害缓解和水资源管理至关重要，但现有深度学习方法在有效利用多样化多媒体数据源方面存在局限，特别是如何协同利用视觉雷达图像和数值气象站测量数据。

Method: 提出M3R架构，包含：1）异质气象数据时间对齐的完整流程；2）专门的多模态注意力机制，以气象站时间序列作为查询，选择性关注空间雷达特征；3）协同结合NEXRAD雷达图像和个人气象站测量数据。

Result: 在三个100km×100km的NEXRAD雷达站中心区域实验中，M3R在准确性、效率和降水检测能力方面均优于现有方法，实现了显著改进。

Conclusion: M3R为基于多媒体的降水临近预报设立了新基准，为业务天气预报系统提供了实用工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate and timely rainfall nowcasting is crucial for disaster mitigation and water resource management. Despite recent advances in deep learning, precipitation prediction remains challenging due to limitations in effectively leveraging diverse multimedia data sources. We introduce M3R, a Meteorology-informed MultiModal attention-based architecture for direct Rainfall prediction that synergistically combines visual NEXRAD radar imagery with numerical Personal Weather Station (PWS) measurements, using a comprehensive pipeline for temporal alignment of heterogeneous meteorological data. With specialized multimodal attention mechanisms, M3R novelly leverages weather station time series as queries to selectively attend to spatial radar features, enabling focused extraction of precipitation signatures. Experimental results for three spatial areas of 100 km * 100 km centered at NEXRAD radar stations demonstrate that M3R outperforms existing approaches, achieving substantial improvements in accuracy, efficiency, and precipitation detection capabilities. Our work establishes new benchmarks for multimedia-based precipitation nowcasting and provides practical tools for operational weather prediction systems. The source code is available at https://github.com/Sanjeev97/M3Rain

</details>


### [64] [Hallucination as Trajectory Commitment: Causal Evidence for Asymmetric Attractor Dynamics in Transformer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15400)
*G. Aytug Akarlar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现自回归语言模型中的幻觉是由早期轨迹承诺和不对称吸引子动力学控制的，表现为快速进入幻觉状态但需要多步协调干预才能纠正


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自回归语言模型中幻觉现象的根本机制，理解为什么模型会快速陷入幻觉状态且难以纠正

Method: 使用相同提示分叉实验观察自发分歧，通过激活修补技术分析因果不对称性，采用窗口修补和残差状态探测分析轨迹动力学

Result: 44.3%的提示在第一生成token处发生分叉；幻觉激活注入正确轨迹的破坏率87.5%，反向恢复率仅33.3%；step-0残差状态能预测幻觉率(r=0.776)；发现五个类似机制的集群

Conclusion: 幻觉是局部稳定的吸引子盆地：进入是概率性且快速的，退出需要跨层和跨步的协调干预，相关盆地在提示编码时已通过可聚类机制确定

Abstract: We present causal evidence that hallucination in autoregressive language models is an early trajectory commitment governed by asymmetric attractor dynamics. Using same-prompt bifurcation, in which we repeatedly sample identical inputs to observe spontaneous divergence, we isolate trajectory dynamics from prompt-level confounds. On Qwen2.5-1.5B across 61 prompts spanning six categories, 27 prompts (44.3%) bifurcate with factual and hallucinated trajectories diverging at the first generated token (KL = 0 at step 0, KL > 1.0 at step 1). Activation patching across 28 layers reveals a pronounced causal asymmetry: injecting a hallucinated activation into a correct trajectory corrupts output in 87.5% of trials (layer 20), while the reverse recovers only 33.3% (layer 24); both exceed the 10.4% baseline (p = 0.025) and 12.5% random-patch control. Window patching shows correction requires sustained multi-step intervention, whereas corruption needs only a single perturbation. Probing the prompt encoding itself, step-0 residual states predict per-prompt hallucination rate at Pearson r = 0.776 at layer 15 (p < 0.001 against a 1000-permutation null); unsupervised clustering identifies five regime-like groups (eta^2 = 0.55) whose saddle-adjacent cluster concentrates 12 of the 13 bifurcating false-premise prompts, indicating that the basin structure is organized around regime commitments fixed at prompt encoding. These findings characterize hallucination as a locally stable attractor basin: entry is probabilistic and rapid, exit demands coordinated intervention across layers and steps, and the relevant basins are selected by clusterable regimes already discernible at step 0.

</details>


### [65] [Dispatch-Aware Ragged Attention for Pruned Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15408)
*Saif Mahmoud,Ahmad Almasri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种轻量级双向Triton注意力内核，解决了Vision Transformers中token修剪后因调度开销导致的延迟瓶颈问题，实现了修剪节省在实际运行时间中的可见性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers中的token修剪方法理论上能通过丢弃非信息性图像块来二次减少注意力计算量，但实际使用现有变长注意力API（如FlashAttention-2 varlen和PyTorch NestedTensor SDPA）时，修剪后序列的注意力延迟并未按预期缩放。研究发现这是由于调度开销瓶颈：在ViTs典型的修剪后短序列长度（≤197个token）下，实际矩阵运算仅需微秒级时间，而主机端调度路径却消耗60-90微秒，导致修剪节省无法在真实运行时间中体现。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的双向Triton注意力内核，其调度开销仅为40微秒，比FlashAttention-2 varlen低约1.5倍。该方法集成到一个完整的pack-attend-unpack流程中，包括序列打包、注意力计算和解包三个步骤，确保修剪节省能在实际运行时间中体现。

Result: 在四种修剪算法（Threshold-L2、DynamicViT、EViT、ATS）上，该系统相比填充的PyTorch SDPA实现了最高2.24倍的端到端吞吐量提升，在DeiT-T/S/B模型上均能有效扩展，并保持比特精确的分类预测，最大绝对logit差异小于0.007。

Conclusion: 通过降低注意力内核的调度开销，使得Vision Transformers中token修剪的计算节省能够在真实运行时间中体现，为高效ViT推理提供了实用解决方案，特别是在短序列场景下调度开销成为主要瓶颈时。

Abstract: Token pruning methods for Vision Transformers (ViTs) promise quadratic reductions in attention FLOPs by dropping uninformative patches. Yet when pruned sequences are executed with state-of-the-art variable-length attention APIs -- including FlashAttention-2's varlen and PyTorch's NestedTensor SDPA-the wall-clock attention latency doesn't scale accordingly. We trace this to a dispatch-overhead bottleneck: at the short, post-pruning sequence lengths typical of ViTs (<=197 tokens), actual matrix arithmetic completes in single-digit microseconds while the host-side dispatch path consumes 60-90 us. We present a lightweight, bidirectional Triton attention kernel whose dispatch floor is 40 us roughly 1.5x lower than FlashAttention-2 varlen-allowing pruning savings to become more visible in wall-clock time. Integrated into a complete pack-attend-unpack pipeline, our system achieves up to 2.24x end-to-end throughput over padded PyTorch SDPA consistently across four pruning algorithms (Threshold-L2, DynamicViT, EViT, ATS), scales across DeiT-T/S/B, and maintains bit-exact classification predictions with <0.007 max absolute logit difference.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Single-Model Optimization: Preserving Plasticity in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15414)
*Lute Lillo,Nick Cheney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeLAPA框架通过构建行为多样化的策略档案库和共享潜在空间，解决持续强化学习中单一策略保存导致的塑性丧失问题，实现更有效的跨任务知识重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续强化学习方法依赖单一策略保存，即使保留先前成功的策略，在任务干扰后也可能无法提供可靠的快速适应起点，存在塑性丧失问题。需要从保留孤立解决方案转向维护技能对齐的策略邻域。

Method: 提出TeLAPA框架，受质量-多样性方法启发：1)为每个任务构建行为多样化的策略档案库；2)维护共享潜在空间，使归档策略在非平稳漂移下保持可比性和可重用性；3)组织策略邻域支持未来再学习。

Result: 在MiniGrid CL设置中，TeLAPA成功学习更多任务，在干扰后重新访问任务时恢复能力更快，在任务序列中保持更高性能。分析显示源最优策略通常不是转移最优策略，有效重用依赖于保留和选择多个邻近替代方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果将持续强化学习重新框架为围绕可重用和胜任的策略邻域，提供了超越单一模型保存、实现更具塑性终身智能体的路径。强调保留多个邻近替代方案而非压缩为单一代表性策略的重要性。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning must balance retention with adaptation, yet many methods still rely on \emph{single-model preservation}, committing to one evolving policy as the main reusable solution across tasks. Even when a previously successful policy is retained, it may no longer provide a reliable starting point for rapid adaptation after interference, reflecting a form of \emph{loss of plasticity} that single-policy preservation cannot address. Inspired by quality-diversity methods, we introduce \textsc{TeLAPA} (Transfer-Enabled Latent-Aligned Policy Archives), a continual RL framework that organizes behaviorally diverse policy neighborhoods into per-task archives and maintains a shared latent space so that archived policies remain comparable and reusable under non-stationary drift. This perspective shifts continual RL from retaining isolated solutions to maintaining \emph{skill-aligned neighborhoods} with competent and behaviorally related policies that support future relearning. In our MiniGrid CL setting, \textsc{TeLAPA} learns more tasks successfully, recovers competence faster on revisited tasks after interference, and retains higher performance across a sequence of tasks. Our analyses show that source-optimal policies are often not transfer-optimal, even within a local competent neighborhood, and that effective reuse depends on retaining and selecting among multiple nearby alternatives rather than collapsing them to one representative. Together, these results reframe continual RL around reusable and competent policy neighborhoods, providing a route beyond single-model preservation toward more plastic lifelong agents.

</details>


### [67] [StoSignSGD: Unbiased Structural Stochasticity Fixes SignSGD for Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15416)
*Dingzhi Yu,Rui Pan,Yuxing Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StoSignSGD算法，通过注入结构性随机性解决SignSGD在非光滑目标上的发散问题，在凸优化中达到最优收敛率，在非凸非光滑优化中改进复杂度界限，并在LLM训练中表现出卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SignSGD等符号优化算法在分布式学习和大型基础模型训练中表现出色，但在ReLU、max-pool、混合专家等现代机器学习常见的非光滑目标上会发散，需要解决这一根本限制。

Method: 提出StoSignSGD算法，在符号算子中注入结构性随机性，同时保持无偏更新步骤。开发符号转换框架，可将任何通用优化器转换为其无偏的基于符号的对应版本。

Result: 理论分析显示：在凸优化中，StoSignSGD解决了SignSGD的非收敛问题，达到匹配下界的最优收敛率；在非凸非光滑优化中，改进了已知复杂度界限。实证结果：在FP8低精度预训练中，比基线加速1.44-2.14倍且保持稳定；在7B LLM数学推理微调中，性能优于AdamW和SignSGD。

Conclusion: StoSignSGD通过结构性随机性注入解决了SignSGD在非光滑目标上的发散问题，在理论和实证上都表现出优越性能，为符号优化算法提供了稳健的解决方案，并在大规模语言模型训练中展现出实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in distributed learning and training large foundation models. Despite their empirical superiority, SignSGD is known to diverge on non-smooth objectives, which are ubiquitous in modern machine learning due to ReLUs, max-pools, and mixture-of-experts. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose \textbf{StoSignSGD}, an algorithm that injects structural stochasticity into the sign operator while maintaining an unbiased update step. In the regime of (online) convex optimization, our theoretical analysis shows that StoSignSGD rigorously resolves the non-convergence issues of SignSGD, achieving a sharp convergence rate matching the lower bound. For the more challenging non-convex non-smooth optimization, we introduce generalized stationary measures that encompass prior definitions, proving that StoSignSGD improves upon the best-known complexity bounds by dimensional factors. Empirically, StoSignSGD exhibits robust stability and superior efficiency across diverse large language model (LLM) training regimes. Notably, in low-precision FP8 pretraining -- a setting where AdamW fails catastrophically -- StoSignSGD remains highly stable and yields a remarkable 1.44$\times$ to 2.14$\times$ speedup relative to established baselines. Furthermore, when fine-tuning 7B LLMs on mathematical reasoning tasks, StoSignSGD delivers substantial performance gains over both AdamW and SignSGD. Finally, to dissect the mechanisms driving its success, we develop a sign conversion framework capable of transforming any general optimizer into its unbiased, sign-based counterpart. Utilizing this framework, we deconstruct the core components of StoSignSGD and present a comprehensive ablation study to empirically validate our algorithmic design choices.

</details>


### [68] [Transfer Learning from Foundational Optimization Embeddings to Unsupervised SAT Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15448)
*Koyena Pal,Serdar Kadioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将基础优化嵌入应用于布尔可满足性问题，展示了从混合整数规划到约束满足领域的跨领域迁移能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基础优化嵌入是否能推广到决策问题，特别是布尔可满足性问题，探索优化与决策问题的统一表示框架

Method: 将CNF公式映射到与MIP相同的二分约束-变量图表示，直接重用预训练嵌入模型，无需架构修改或有监督微调

Result: 嵌入能够捕捉SAT实例的结构规律，支持无监督任务如实例聚类和分布识别，首次证明基础优化嵌入可迁移到约束满足领域

Conclusion: 基础优化嵌入可推广到决策问题，为实现优化与决策问题的统一表示框架迈出重要一步

Abstract: Foundational optimization embeddings have recently emerged as powerful pre-trained representations for mixed-integer programming (MIP) problems. These embeddings were shown to enable cross-domain transfer and reduce reliance on solver-generated labels. In this work, we investigate whether such representations generalize beyond optimization to decision problems, focusing on Boolean satisfiability (SAT). We adapt the foundational optimization architecture to SAT by mapping CNF formulas into the same bipartite constraint-variable graph representation used for MIPs. This allows direct reuse of the pre-trained embedding model without architectural changes or supervised fine-tuning. Our results show that these embeddings capture structural regularities in SAT instances and support unsupervised tasks such as instance clustering and distribution identification. We demonstrate, for the first time, that foundational optimization embeddings can transfer to constraint satisfaction domains. Our findings is a step toward a unified representational framework for both optimization and decision problems.

</details>


### [69] [Harmonizing Multi-Objective LLM Unlearning via Unified Domain Representation and Bidirectional Logit Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15482)
*Yisheng Zhong,Sijia Liu,Zhuangdi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多目标LLM遗忘框架，通过数据和优化协同设计平衡遗忘有害知识、保持通用能力、避免过度拒绝邻域概念和增强对抗鲁棒性四个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法主要关注遗忘效果和效用保持，忽视了对抗鲁棒性和边界行为，且多目标设置下存在任务干扰问题，需要一种能同时满足多个挑战性目标的统一框架。

Method: 采用数据和优化协同设计：1）将训练语料标准化为统一数据表示以减少领域差距；2）引入双向蒸馏方法，从上下文指导的教师模型中引导期望行为，同时抑制学生模型中的不良行为。

Result: 理论和实证分析表明该方法能对齐领域分布，将看似无关的遗忘任务转化为协同优化，在多样化挑战性需求下实现平衡可靠的遗忘，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 提出的多目标遗忘框架通过统一数据表示和双向蒸馏，有效解决了LLM遗忘中的多目标协调问题，实现了遗忘效果、效用保持、边界行为和对抗鲁棒性的平衡优化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) unlearning is crucial for removing hazardous or privacy-leaking information from the model. Practical LLM unlearning demands satisfying multiple challenging objectives simultaneously: removing undesirable knowledge, preserving general utility, avoiding over-refusal of neighboring concepts, and, crucially, ensuring robustness against adversarial probing attacks. However, existing unlearning methods primarily focus on a limited subset of these goals, typically unlearning efficacy and utility preservation while overlooking robustness and boundary behaviors. Naively extending these methods to multi-objective settings may lead to unlearning task interference. We propose a novel multi-objective unlearning framework that harmonizes multiple unlearning objectives through a data and optimization co-design: We standardize training corpora into a unified data representation to reduce the domain gap, and then introduce a bidirectional distillation method that simultaneously elicits desired behavior from a context-instructed teacher while suppressing undesirable behavior in the student model. Theoretical and empirical analyses show that our method aligns domain distributions and converts seemingly irrelevant unlearning tasks into cooperative optimization. Evaluation demonstrates state-of-the-art performance, which enables balanced and reliable unlearning across diverse, challenging requirements.

</details>


### [70] [$π_{0.7}$: a Steerable Generalist Robotic Foundation Model with Emergent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15483)
*Physical Intelligence,Bo Ai,Ali Amin,Raichelle Aniceto,Ashwin Balakrishna,Greg Balke,Kevin Black,George Bokinsky,Shihao Cao,Thomas Charbonnier,Vedant Choudhary,Foster Collins,Ken Conley,Grace Connors,James Darpinian,Karan Dhabalia,Maitrayee Dhaka,Jared DiCarlo,Danny Driess,Michael Equi,Adnan Esmail,Yunhao Fang,Chelsea Finn,Catherine Glossop,Thomas Godden,Ivan Goryachev,Lachlan Groom,Haroun Habeeb,Hunter Hancock,Karol Hausman,Gashon Hussein,Victor Hwang,Brian Ichter,Connor Jacobsen,Szymon Jakubczak,Rowan Jen,Tim Jones,Gregg Kammerer,Ben Katz,Liyiming Ke,Mairbek Khadikov,Chandra Kuchi,Marinda Lamb,Devin LeBlanc,Brendon LeCount,Sergey Levine,Xinyu Li,Adrian Li-Bell,Vladislav Lialin,Zhonglin Liang,Wallace Lim,Yao Lu,Enyu Luo,Vishnu Mano,Nandan Marwaha,Aikys Mongush,Liam Murphy,Suraj Nair,Tyler Patterson,Karl Pertsch,Allen Z. Ren,Gavin Schelske,Charvi Sharma,Baifeng Shi,Lucy Xiaoyang Shi,Laura Smith,Jost Tobias Springenberg,Kyle Stachowicz,Will Stoeckle,Jiaming Tang,Jimmy Tanner,Shalom Tekeste,Marcel Torne,Kyle Vedder,Quan Vuong,Anna Walling,Haohuan Wang,Jason Wang,XuDong Wang,Chris Whalen,Samuel Whitmore,Blake Williams,Charles Xu,Sukwon Yoo,Lili Yu,Wuming Zhang,Zhuoyang Zhang,Ury Zhilinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: π₀.₇是一个新的机器人基础模型，通过多样化上下文条件训练实现强大的开箱即用性能，支持多阶段任务、跨具身泛化和复杂操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个通用的机器人基础模型，能够在未见过的环境中执行多样化语言指令，实现零样本跨具身泛化，并在复杂任务上达到与专门RL微调模型相当的性能

Method: 采用多样化上下文条件训练方法，模型不仅基于语言指令，还基于描述执行方式和策略的多模态信息（包括任务性能元数据和子目标图像）进行条件化，从而能够利用包括演示、潜在次优数据（包括失败数据）和非机器人来源数据在内的多样化数据

Result: π₀.₇在多种机器人平台上评估，在需要速度和灵巧性、语言跟随和组合任务泛化的任务中表现出色，能够执行多阶段厨房任务、零样本折叠衣物、操作意式咖啡机等复杂任务，性能与专门RL微调模型相当

Conclusion: 通过多样化上下文条件训练构建的π₀.₇模型展示了强大的开箱即用性能和泛化能力，为机器人基础模型的发展提供了新方向，能够处理复杂、多样化的现实世界任务

Abstract: We present a new robotic foundation model, called $π_{0.7}$, that can enable strong out-of-the-box performance in a wide range of scenarios. $π_{0.7}$ can follow diverse language instructions in unseen environments, including multi-stage tasks with various kitchen appliances, provide zero-shot cross-embodiment generalization, for example enabling a robot to fold laundry without seeing the task before, and perform challenging tasks such as operating an espresso machine out of the box at a level of performance that matches much more specialized RL-finetuned models. The main idea behind $π_{0.7}$ is to use diverse context conditioning during training. This conditioning information, contained in the prompt, makes it possible to steer the model precisely to perform many tasks with different strategies. It is conditioned not just on a language command that describes what it should do, but on additional multimodal information that also describes the manner or strategy in which it should do it, including metadata about task performance and subgoal images. This enables $π_{0.7}$ to use very diverse data, including demonstrations, potentially suboptimal (autonomous) data including failures, and data from non-robot sources. Our experiments evaluate $π_{0.7}$ across numerous tasks with multiple robot platforms, on tasks that require speed and dexterity, language following, and compositional task generalization.

</details>


### [71] [FineSteer: A Unified Framework for Fine-Grained Inference-Time Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15488)
*Zixuan Weng,Jinghuai Zhang,Kunlin Cai,Ying Li,Peiran Wang,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FineSteer是一个新颖的推理时引导框架，通过条件引导和细粒度向量合成两阶段设计，实现对LLM行为的精细控制，在保持模型效用的同时提升安全性和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法存在三个主要问题：1) 效果有限；2) 难以保持模型通用能力（效用损失）；3) 训练效率低。这些方法采用僵化的"一刀切"设计，缺乏适应性，无法在有效引导、保持效用和训练效率之间取得平衡。

Method: FineSteer采用两阶段框架：1) 子空间引导条件引导(SCS)：通过条件判断避免不必要的引导，保护模型效用；2) 混合引导专家(MoSE)：捕捉期望引导行为的多模态特性，生成查询特定的引导向量。SCS包含引导条件判断和子空间投影，MoSE采用专家混合架构学习不同引导模式。

Result: 在安全和真实性基准测试中，FineSteer在整体性能上优于现有最先进方法，实现了更强的引导性能且效用损失最小。具体表现为：1) 在安全性任务中有效减少有害输出；2) 在真实性任务中减少幻觉；3) 在一般查询上保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: FineSteer通过创新的两阶段设计解决了现有推理时引导方法的局限性，实现了效果、效用和训练效率的平衡。该框架为LLM行为控制提供了灵活、自适应的解决方案，在保持模型通用能力的同时有效提升特定行为表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit undesirable behaviors, such as safety violations and hallucinations. Although inference-time steering offers a cost-effective way to adjust model behavior without updating its parameters, existing methods often fail to be simultaneously effective, utility-preserving, and training-efficient due to their rigid, one-size-fits-all designs and limited adaptability. In this work, we present FineSteer, a novel steering framework that decomposes inference-time steering into two complementary stages: conditional steering and fine-grained vector synthesis, allowing fine-grained control over when and how to steer internal representations. In the first stage, we introduce a Subspace-guided Conditional Steering (SCS) mechanism that preserves model utility by avoiding unnecessary steering. In the second stage, we propose a Mixture-of-Steering-Experts (MoSE) mechanism that captures the multimodal nature of desired steering behaviors and generates query-specific steering vectors for improved effectiveness. Through tailored designs in both SCS and MoSE, FineSteer maintains robust performance on general queries while adaptively optimizing steering vectors for targeted inputs in a training-efficient manner. Extensive experiments on safety and truthfulness benchmarks show that FineSteer outperforms state-of-the-art methods in overall performance, achieving stronger steering performance with minimal utility loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YukinoAsuna/FineSteer

</details>


### [72] [Predicting Where Steering Vectors Succeed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15557)
*Jayadev Billa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出线性可访问性剖面(LAP)作为预测导向向量有效性的诊断工具，通过应用解嵌入矩阵到中间隐藏状态来评估概念的可线性访问性，无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前导向向量方法在某些概念和层有效，但在其他情况下失败，且实践者无法在干预前预测哪种设置适用，需要一种预测导向向量有效性的诊断方法。

Method: 引入线性可访问性剖面(LAP)，将logit lens重新用作导向向量有效性的预测器。关键度量A_lin应用模型的解嵌入矩阵到中间隐藏状态，无需训练。在5个模型(Pythia-2.8B到Llama-8B)的24个受控二元概念族上进行评估。

Result: 峰值A_lin预测导向有效性达到ρ=+0.86到+0.91，层选择预测达到ρ=+0.63到+0.92。三阶段框架解释了何时均值差导向有效、何时需要非线性方法、何时任何方法都无效。实体导向演示确认了端到端预测：在LAP推荐层导向可重定向Gemma-2-2B和OLMo-2-1B-Instruct的补全，而中间层(标准启发式)对两个模型都无效。

Conclusion: LAP提供了一种无需训练的诊断工具，可预测导向向量的有效性，解决了实践者无法预判导向向量适用性的问题，并通过三阶段框架为不同情况提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Steering vectors work for some concepts and layers but fail for others, and practitioners have no way to predict which setting applies before running an intervention. We introduce the Linear Accessibility Profile (LAP), a per-layer diagnostic that repurposes the logit lens as a predictor of steering vector effectiveness. The key measure, $A_{\mathrm{lin}}$, applies the model's unembedding matrix to intermediate hidden states, requiring no training. Across 24 controlled binary concept families on five models (Pythia-2.8B to Llama-8B), peak $A_{\mathrm{lin}}$ predicts steering effectiveness at $ρ= +0.86$ to $+0.91$ and layer selection at $ρ= +0.63$ to $+0.92$. A three-regime framework explains when difference-of-means steering works, when nonlinear methods are needed, and when no method can work. An entity-steering demo confirms the prediction end-to-end: steering at the LAP-recommended layer redirects completions on Gemma-2-2B and OLMo-2-1B-Instruct, while the middle layer (the standard heuristic) has no effect on either model.

</details>


### [73] [Reward Weighted Classifier-Free Guidance as Policy Improvement in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15577)
*Alexander Peysakhovich,William Berman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出奖励加权分类器自由引导（RCFG）作为策略改进算子，可在测试时优化新奖励函数，无需重新训练模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过强化学习在训练时调整模型采样分布以增加奖励，但当奖励函数改变时需要重新训练，这限制了灵活性和效率

Method: 提出奖励加权分类器自由引导（RCFG），作为策略改进算子，近似通过Q函数倾斜采样分布，可在测试时优化新奖励函数

Result: 在分子生成任务中证明RCFG可在测试时优化新奖励函数；将RCFG作为教师蒸馏到基础策略中，显著加速标准强化学习的收敛

Conclusion: RCFG提供了一种无需重新训练即可在测试时适应新奖励函数的灵活方法，同时可作为强化学习的有效预热策略

Abstract: Consider an auto-regressive model that produces outputs x (e.g., answers to questions, molecules) each of which can be summarized by an attribute vector y (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness, or bio-availability vs. lipophilicity). An arbitrary reward function r(y) encodes tradeoffs between these properties. Typically, tilting the model's sampling distribution to increase this reward is done at training time via reinforcement learning. However, if the reward function changes, re-alignment requires re-training. In this paper, we show that a reward weighted classifier-free guidance (RCFG) can act as a policy improvement operator in this setting, approximating tilting the sampling distribution by the Q function. We apply RCFG to molecular generation, demonstrating that it can optimize novel reward functions at test time. Finally, we show that using RCFG as a teacher and distilling into the base policy to serve as a warm start significantly speeds up convergence for standard RL.

</details>


### [74] [PAWN: Piece Value Analysis with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15585)
*Ethan Tang,Hasan Davulcu,Jia Zou,Zhongju Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用CNN自编码器提取棋盘潜在位置表示，结合MLP架构显著提升国际象棋棋子相对价值预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测国际象棋棋子在特定位置中的相对价值是一个开放挑战，因为棋子的贡献取决于其与棋盘上所有其他棋子的空间关系。传统方法缺乏对完整棋盘状态的考虑。

Method: 采用基于CNN的自编码器提取棋盘潜在位置表示，将其作为上下文信息输入到MLP架构中。使用超过1200万个从特级大师对局中收集的棋子-价值对数据集，通过Stockfish 17生成真实标签。

Result: 增强后的棋子价值预测器显著优于无上下文MLP系统，验证集平均绝对误差降低16%，相对棋子价值预测误差约为0.65个兵。这表明编码完整问题状态作为上下文能为预测个体组件贡献提供有用的归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 通过CNN自编码器编码完整棋盘状态显著提高了棋子价值预测的准确性，证明了在预测个体组件贡献时考虑完整问题状态的重要性。

Abstract: Predicting the relative value of any given chess piece in a position remains an open challenge, as a piece's contribution depends on its spatial relationships with every other piece on the board. We demonstrate that incorporating the state of the full chess board via latent position representations derived using a CNN-based autoencoder significantly improves accuracy for MLP-based piece value prediction architectures. Using a dataset of over 12 million piece-value pairs gathered from Grandmaster-level games, with ground-truth labels generated by Stockfish 17, our enhanced piece value predictor significantly outperforms context-independent MLP-based systems, reducing validation mean absolute error by 16% and predicting relative piece value within approximately 0.65 pawns. More generally, our findings suggest that encoding the full problem state as context provides useful inductive bias for predicting the contribution of any individual component.

</details>


### [75] [Adapting in the Dark: Efficient and Stable Test-Time Adaptation for Black-Box Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15609)
*Yunbei Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Chengyi Cai,Feng Liu,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BETA框架通过轻量级本地白盒引导模型为黑盒API模型创建可处理的梯度路径，实现无需额外API调用的高效测试时自适应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型（仅通过API访问）的测试时自适应（TTA）研究不足。现有方法如后处理输出细化适应性有限，而零阶优化（ZOO）面临高查询成本和优化挑战

Method: 使用轻量级本地白盒引导模型创建可处理的梯度路径，结合预测协调技术、一致性正则化和提示学习导向的过滤，实现稳定自适应

Result: 在ImageNet-C上，ViT-B/16准确率提升7.1%，CLIP提升3.4%，超越TENT和TPT等白盒/灰盒方法；在商业API上，以250倍低成本达到ZOO相当性能，保持实时推理速度

Conclusion: BETA为现实世界黑盒TTA提供了实用高效的解决方案，无需额外API调用且延迟可忽略，显著降低了查询成本

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) for black-box models accessible only via APIs remains a largely unexplored challenge. Existing approaches such as post-hoc output refinement offer limited adaptive capacity, while Zeroth-Order Optimization (ZOO) enables input-space adaptation but faces high query costs and optimization challenges in the unsupervised TTA setting. We introduce BETA (Black-box Efficient Test-time Adaptation), a framework that addresses these limitations by employing a lightweight, local white-box steering model to create a tractable gradient pathway. Through a prediction harmonization technique combined with consistency regularization and prompt learning-oriented filtering, BETA enables stable adaptation with no additional API calls and negligible latency beyond standard inference. On ImageNet-C, BETA achieves a +7.1% accuracy gain on ViT-B/16 and +3.4% on CLIP, surpassing strong white-box and gray-box methods including TENT and TPT. On a commercial API, BETA achieves comparable performance to ZOO at 250x lower cost while maintaining real-time inference speed, establishing it as a practical and efficient solution for real-world black-box TTA.

</details>


### [76] [Flexible Empowerment at Reasoning with Extended Best-of-N Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15614)
*Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合赋能在强化学习中推理动作的新方法，通过扩展的BoN采样技术灵活调整探索-利用困境平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将赋能作为内在奖励项加入任务奖励函数，导致策略学习延迟且难以动态调整探索程度。BoN采样技术能够在不显式学习策略的情况下获得修改后的策略，有望更灵活地调整探索-利用平衡

Method: 提出基于Tsalis统计扩展的BoN采样方法，将赋能纳入动作推理过程，通过统计扩展实现可泛化的策略修改程度调整，同时保持计算成本可控

Result: 在玩具问题上验证了方法平衡探索-利用困境的能力，并在复杂运动任务中展示了强化学习性能的提升

Conclusion: 通过将BoN采样技术应用于赋能，实现了更灵活的探索-利用平衡调整，扩展的Tsalis统计方法提供了可泛化的策略修改程度控制，在复杂任务中提升了强化学习性能

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel method that incorporates empowerment when reasoning actions in reinforcement learning (RL), thereby achieving the flexibility of exploration-exploitation dilemma (EED). In previous methods, empowerment for promoting exploration has been provided as a bonus term to the task-specific reward function as an intrinsically-motivated RL. However, this approach introduces a delay until the policy that accounts for empowerment is learned, making it difficult to adjust the emphasis on exploration as needed. On the other hand, a trick devised for fine-tuning recent foundation models at reasoning, so-called best-of-N (BoN) sampling, allows for the implicit acquisition of modified policies without explicitly learning them. It is expected that applying this trick to exploration-promoting terms, such as empowerment, will enable more flexible adjustment of EED. Therefore, this paper investigates BoN sampling for empowerment. Furthermore, to adjust the degree of policy modification in a generalizable manner while maintaining computational cost, this paper proposes a novel BoN sampling method extended by Tsalis statistics. Through toy problems, the proposed method's cability to balance EED is verified. In addition, it is demonstrated that the proposed method improves RL performance to solve complex locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [77] [Majority Voting for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15618)
*Tim Launer,Jonas Hübotter,Marco Bagatella,Ido Hakimi,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FMV通过运行时代码执行签名从多个生成中识别代表性解决方案，显著提升代码生成性能，并可作为无标签测试时强化学习的聚合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于功能共识的代码生成方法，探索如何通过测试时推理策略提升大语言模型在代码生成任务上的性能，同时扩展功能共识在无标签测试时强化学习中的应用。

Method: 提出Functional Majority Voting (FMV)方法，基于多个生成代码在测试输入上的运行执行签名来识别代表性解决方案；将该方法扩展为无标签测试时强化学习的聚合策略。

Result: FMV作为测试时推理策略显著提升了LiveCodeBench上的性能且计算开销较小；作为无标签测试时强化学习的聚合策略提高了holdout任务上的pass@1，但未发现超越基础模型性能上限的自我改进证据。

Conclusion: FMV是有效的代码生成测试时推理方法，功能共识可扩展应用于无标签测试时强化学习，但需要进一步研究如何突破基础模型的性能上限。

Abstract: We investigate Functional Majority Voting (FMV), a method based on functional consensus for code generation with Large Language Models, which identifies a representative solution from multiple generations using their runtime execution signatures on test inputs. We find that FMV is an effective test-time inference strategy, substantially boosting performance on LiveCodeBench without a large compute overhead. Furthermore, we extend the utility of functional consensus and apply it as an aggregation strategy for label-free Test-Time Reinforcement Learning.
